d0a41b8c-4e11-ee11-8f6d-6045bdec3050	5c53d2f8-9d12-ee11-8f6e-6045bdf05170	AIC Expert services Basic	AM01924	English	Fortra offers AIC Expert Services to its Customers. The Fortra’s AIC Specialist Consultant will partner with your team and provide a number of services, including but not limited to:       Expert Advice: Assist and advise on configuration changes on the customer environment.       Mentoring and Training: Provide one-to-one training, customized training on product features to strengthen and enable your in-house team to better administer your AIC Solution.       Upgrades and Migration Guidance: Provide best practices approach for upgrades and seamless transition from the current AIC Solution environment to an enterprise environment supported by Fortra.       Performance Services: Recommendations for tuning your AIC Solution and automation workflows.       Optimization Services: Evaluate your business process to identify workflow solutions and increase efficiency and resiliency using AIC Solution.   Services automatically renew for successive twelve (12) month periods unless Client provides written notice of its election to not renew sixty (60) days in advance of the renewal date.   For any Expert Services engagement, you agree to the following:   Unused hours under any plan do not carry over from month to month.  Hours must be used monthly according to your chosen plan  Additional hours above the allotted monthly can be purchased at standard rates.   Minimum 1-hour meetings with 30 min increments  Service requests will be responded to in less than (2) business days.  Operation hours are 8 am - 5 pm local time in supported regions.  Hours per month: Up to 4

d28ce3a8-6a0f-ee11-8f6d-6045bdd9c133	c6fe0b3d-690f-ee11-8f6d-6045bdd9c2e1	AIC QuickStart Services	AM01922	English	Objective Plan, advise, install, configure, validate, and implement an AIC system.   How it Works We will deliver the services in four steps:  1.	Planning: our Technical Consultant will review and discuss the architecture and prerequisites for your implementation. Also, we will discuss your use cases and security requirements for the optimal configuration. We will also discuss the steps needed and dates for your go-live. 2.	Installation and Configuration: This includes activating the license, installation, and configuration of features according to your use cases 3.	Validation: Once the configuration is complete, we will work with you to validate your AIC deployment is working correctly. We will validate our actions with a document sample, validating the database and confirming storage. 4.	Document definition and Handover: Once the AIC installation and configuration are completed by the consultant, we will select a document type from your backlog and up to 4 vendors and create the document definitions. Deliverables •	Planning checklist. •	Tailored installation and configuration of AIC aligned with best practices and optimal settings. •	Service delivery documentation (Administrator document & Implementation report). Scope of Services  The consultant will perform the following tasks:  Pre-Implementation (remote conference and email) •	Meet your Project Manager (your primary contact for implementation) and Implementation Consultant •	Create Project Plan •	Subscribe to the customer portal (Access to latest releases, documentation and support) •	Project Kick-Off •	Verify and review AIC prerequisites and architecture. •	Build AIC server environment. Installation and Setup of AIC environment - 1 server •	Installation of AIC modules and license •	Configure user/access definitions. •	Document discovery session •	Setting up AIC Framework •	Configure AIC Server Defaults •	Setting up folder structure •	Configure AIC History and Audit level. •	Configure Email Trigger •	Configure DB Output •	Configure Folder Trigger     Implementation of 1 type of document (e.g., Invoice, PO, AP, Bank statement) with up to 4 vendors •	Review and confirmation of document examples (requiring on average 30 examples), outputs and definitions. •	Define Document Data Points •	Create Document Types •	Create Document Form Definitions •	Create Batch Types •	Define Export Settings •	Train AI Model •	Testing and AI Model Training Corrections •	Set Automatic Class Verification •	Set Automatic Data Verification •	Document configuration, setup and AIC corrections •	Configure Alerting and Monitoring Methods Hand-over •	One time 2-hour session with AIC Technical Consultant

36775f8b-4e11-ee11-8f6d-6045bdf05170	9eab0083-3c11-ee11-8f6d-6045bdd9c62d	AIC Resident Engineer - Basic	AM01927	English	An AIC Resident Engineer project involves hiring a temporary Automate Intellingence capture professional to supplement your company's existing staff during peak periods or to fill skills gaps. Our consultants could work alongside your existing staff to complete your projects, providing additional capacity and expertise.  This approach allows your company to scale up your workforce quickly and efficiently, without committing to long-term hires. It can also help you to quickly adapt to changing client demands, while maintaining a high level of service and expertise.  Unused hours under any plan do not carry over from month to month.  Hours must be used monthly according to your chosen plan Additional hours above the allotted monthly can be purchased on demand Operation hours are 8 am - 5 pm local time in supported regions.  Up to 40 hours monthly.

d88ce3a8-6a0f-ee11-8f6d-6045bdd9c133	e62d873b-690f-ee11-8f6d-6045bdd9c62d	Automate Desktop QuickStart Services	AM01920	English	Objective Plan, advise, install, configure, validate and implement an Automate Desktop system.   How it Works We will deliver the services in four steps: 1.	Planning: our Technical Consultant will review and discuss the architecture and prerequisites for your implementation. Also, we will discuss your use cases and security requirements for the optimal configuration. We will also discuss the steps needed and dates for your go-live. 2.	Installation and Configuration: This includes activating the license, installation, and configuration of features according to your use cases 3.	Validation: Once the configuration is complete, we will work with you to validate your Automate Desktop deployment is working correctly. We will validate our actions with a sample bot testing internal connectivity 4.	Workshop: Once the Automate Desktop installation and configuration are completed by the consultant, we will select a process from your backlog and work with your administrator in a mentorship program. 4 sessions of 2 hours each will be scheduled to help you build your process following best practices and methodologies. Deliverables •	Tailored installation and configuration of Automate Desktop aligned with best practices and optimal settings. •	Service delivery documentation (Administrator document). Scope of Services  The consultant will perform the following tasks:  Planning (remote conference and email) •	Meet your RPA specialist during a kickoff call. •	Discuss your processes and requirements with the RPA specialist •	Provide detailed information about your systems and processes. •	Help you subscribe to the customer portal (Access to latest releases, documentation and support) •	Help you subscribe to Automate Academy  •	Review necessary licensing of Automate Desktop. •	Validate all prerequisites in a kickoff remote session.  Process discovery •	Select a quick win for your team. •	Estimate the automation effort. •	Generate documentation. •	Evaluate and approve your Automate design, statements of work, and timeline. •	Define and set mentorship sessions. Workshop •	Check in on the status of your implementation during regularly scheduled meetings •	Show you how to organize Tasks (Organized by Application, Server, Platform, Department) •	Help you establish a naming convention following our best practices. •	Set retries and contingencies. •	Help you define who receives notifications for various events in the workflow. •	Add Recovery Instructions Validation and Testing •	Production Readiness and Go-Live Review •	Conduct basic troubleshooting of Automate Desktop configuration for issues related to the service.

cda41b8c-4e11-ee11-8f6d-6045bdec3050	c3cc4a7d-3c11-ee11-8f6d-6045bdd9ca46	Automate Desktop to Plus Training	AM01932	English	Training Agenda Summary:  This training course is intended as a complete progressive guide for the Automate Plus™ and Automate Ultimate™ administrator/developer who have recently upgraded from Automate Desktop. Included in the course is an overview of the core functionality and advanced programming using Automate Plus/Ultimate. The training will focus on developing and implementing workflows and tasks, including log management, error handling and execution management, which may then be tailored to your specific processes.  Who is it intended for?  The course is aimed at new users of Automate Plus™ or Automate Ultimate™, who have prior experience with Automate Desktop. The course aims to equip the trainee with the necessary knowledge and tools to migrate their tasks from Automate Desktop and perform all future automation activities within Automate Plus/Ultimate.  Prerequisites: An intermediate understanding/knowledge of Automate Desktop is required. Good PC skills and software programming would be beneficial but is not essential. A basic understanding/knowledge of SQL, REGex and HTML would also be advantageous but is not essential.

How it Works We will deliver the services in four steps:  1.	Planning: our Technical Consultant will review and discuss the architecture and prerequisites for your implementation. Also, we will discuss your use cases and security requirements for the optimal configuration. We will also discuss the steps needed and dates for your go-live. 2.	Installation and Configuration: This includes activating the license, installation, and configuration of features according to your use cases 3.	Validation: Once the configuration is complete, we will collaborate with you to validate your Automate Plus deployment is working correctly. We will validate our actions with a sample bot testing internal connectivity, validating the database and confirming storage. 4.	Bot building and Handover: Once the Automate Plus installation and configuration are completed by the consultant, we will select a process from your backlog and develop the bot for you.  Deliverables •	Planning checklist. •	Tailored installation and configuration of Automate Plus aligned with best practices and optimal settings. •	Service delivery documentation (Administrator document & Implementation report). Scope of Services  The consultant will perform the following tasks:  Planning (remote conference and email) •	Meet your Project Manager (your primary contact for implementation) and Implementation Consultant •	Discuss your processes and requirements with the Project Manager •	Provide detailed information about your systems and processes. •	Subscribe to the customer portal (Access to latest releases, documentation and support) •	Subscribe to Automate Academy  •	Review necessary licensing of Automate Plus. •	Review and define architecture diagram. •	Review and validate communication to file shares and database. •	Validate access to applications and web portals. •	Validate all prerequisites in a kickoff remote session. Configuration •	Define SMTP Server. •	Set default notification email address. •	Set default Windows Login •	Set “From” Address. •	Create Automate User Definitions •	Define Security Roles within Automate •	Configure AUTOMATE History and Audit level. •	Configure Dashboards.  Process discovery •	Select a quick win for your team. •	Estimate the automation effort. •	Generate documentation. •	Define and set bot building sessions. Implementation    •	Evaluate and approve your Automate design, statements of work, and timeline. •	Check in on the status of your implementation during regularly scheduled meetings. •	Organize Tasks and Workflows (Organized by Application, Server, Platform, Department) •	Establish a naming convention following our best practices. •	Define agent distribution. •	Set retries and contingencies. •	Set Workflow best practices. •	Establish a protocol for moving workflows between environments. •	Knowledge transfer session •	Define who receives notifications for various events in the workflow. •	Add Recovery Instructions •	Pre-Launch Validation and Testing •	Project management •	Production Readiness and Go-Live Review •	Conduct basic troubleshooting of Automate Plus configuration for issues related to the service.

We will also discuss your use cases and security requirements for the optimal configuration. Additionally, we will discuss the steps needed and dates for your go-live implementation. 2. Installation and Configuration: This includes activating the license, installation, and configuration of appliance(s) as well as client defined custom rule configurations for DLP and AV. 3. Validation: Once the installation and configuration are complete, we will work with you to validate that your ICAP deployment is working correctly. We will test and validate the application is scanning file(s) and returning valid results. And finally, we will perform a multi-point inspection checklist for production readiness and go-live. 4. Handover and review: Once the ICAP installation and configuration are completed by the consultant, we will hand it over to you and train your team on basic administration and operations best practices. Deliverables • Planning checklist. • Tailored installation and configuration of ICAP aligned with best practices and optimal settings. • Service delivery documentation (ICAP configuration settings). Scope of Services The consultant will perform the following tasks: Planning • Review necessary licensing of ICAP Gateway • Review and validate firewall rules necessary for scanning files • Review and validate communication to GoAnywhere MFT Application • Validate all prerequisites in a pre-check remote session. 40029f5c-0d01-ec11-811a-0050569f6bb1	c7e51445-bff6-eb11-811a-0050569f6bb1	FileCatalyst Central Migration Services	FC09019	English	FileCatalyst Central Migration Service  Objective  Provide a one-time migration for one (1) configuration of each applicable FileCatalyst Server application from a pre-installed FileCatalyst Server (“FC Source”) into a pre-installed FileCatalyst Server (“FC Target”).    Deliverables  • Planning checklist document. • One-time Implementation session. Installation in the target environment, migration of configuration from the source environment to target environment.  • One-time handoff session. • Service delivery documentation (Architecture Diagram, configuration report).  Scope of Services   The consultant will perform the following tasks:   Planning (remote conference and email)  • Validate all prerequisites in a pre-check remote session.  • Review necessary licensing of FileCatalyst Servers and provide perpetual license for Target environment.  • Review and validate firewall rules necessary for a secured file transfer for external and internal communication.  • Review and validate communication to file shares and database (if applicable).  • Validate path access to file share locations for internal and external users.   Implementation     • Install applicable software applications in the Target environment (see chart below).  • Register/Activate perpetual license.  • Migrate User configuration for user defaults, groups, folders, Active Directory settings, security policies, and Web Branding (Workflow only).  • Reconfigure folder paths to new storage (if applicable).  • Provide perpetual licensing for all components, which enables all purchased features.  • Install and migrate FileCatalyst Central for one (1) license.  • Configure all applications for communication with target FileCatalyst Central.  • Install and migrate FileCatalyst Load Balancer for one (1) license.   Validation and Testing  • Test and validate connectivity in the new FC target.  • Validate Internal and External communications are open and working.  • Validate Load Balancing for the FC Targets (if applicable).    Final Review  • Go through final review of installation and verify setup / configuration.  • Go through security review of applicable components (e.g., TLS, SSL).  • Work with the customer team to define and go through testing for internal and external communications.  • Provide current process and workflows review customer’s automation settings to look for and provide best practices (if applicable).   Requirements  • Perform User Acceptance Testing (“UAT”) on a separate non-production environment (e.g. test or pre-production) is highly recommended before routing FC traffic from the FC Source to the FC Target. The customer is responsible for performing UAT activities unless UAT services are purchased.  • Change freeze on FC source configuration is required as soon as the migration service starts and until all FC traffic is routed to the FC target. The customer is responsible for maintaining any FC configuration changes in both FC source and FC target during the change freeze period. The Customer may opt to cutover to the FC target without the configuration changes and address any situation it might have, or the customer will need to purchase additional migrations services to re-do the migration if the change freeze is not maintained.  • The customer is responsible for coordinating and performing cutover activities to route traffic from FC source to the FC target system after the UAT activities have finished unless the Customer purchases FileCatalyst Cutover Assistance services for such work.   Assumptions   • The customer will route all traffic from FC Source to FC Target by the end of the implementation session.  • The customer may contact Fortra Technical Support for any issues found after the implementation session unless a dedicated cutover assistance service is purchased.  • Fortra is not responsible for any connectivity issues with non-FileCatalyst systems. Fortra highly recommends UAT to minimize possible connectivity issues with other non-FileCatalyst systems.

Review of the upgrade strategy plan, risks, and compatibility.   Deliverables  • Planning sessions (remote conference and email).  • One-time Implementation session (after-hours included).  • Upgrade of software for one (1) of each applicable FileCatalyst license.  • Post-upgrade validation checklist.   Scope of Services   The consultant will perform the following tasks:   Planning   • Plan, discuss, and review a strategy plan, risks, and compatibility for the FileCatalyst upgrade. (1 hour)   Implementation    • Backup of all applicable configurations.  • Software upgrade for one (1) of each applicable FileCatalyst license and client.  • Upgrade of one (1) FileCatalyst Central license to the latest version.  • Upgrade of one (1) FileCatalyst Load balancer license to the latest version.   Validation and Testing  • Validate connectivity between FileCatalyst applications.  • Perform FileCatalyst pre-defined validation & testing procedures.  • Test all protocols connectivity and validate reporting is working.  • Conduct basic troubleshooting of the FileCatalyst configuration for issues related to the service.  • Assist with up to one (1) rollback to a previously implemented version of FileCatalyst (if needed).   Requirements  • Perform User Acceptance Testing (“UAT”) on a separate FileCatalyst environment (e.g., test or pre-production) is highly recommended before routing traffic from existing FileCatalyst deployment to new FileCatalyst deployment. The c is responsible for performing UAT activities unless UAT services are purchased.  • Change freeze on FileCatalyst application configuration is required as soon as the upgrade service starts until the upgrade has finished with all FileCatalyst related traffic routed to the New Deployment. The customer is responsible for maintaining any configuration changes in both server and clients during the change freeze period.   • The customer is responsible for coordinating and performing cutover activities to route traffic from existing FileCatalyst deployment to new FileCatalyst deployment after the UAT activities have finished unless the customer purchases a cutover assistance service.   Assumptions   • The upgrade service assumes that the customer must reuse the same architecture and infrastructure such as hypervisor virtual machines for on-prem, or cloud provider for cloud, file storage, firewalls, Active Directory, service accounts, database, and network) used by the FileCatalyst deployment and applicable access (usernames and passwords) for each component available.  • Fortra will not perform any configuration changes such as re-configuration of folder paths, ports tasks, custom commands, post-task scripts; automation refactoring, configuration clean up as part of the Upgrade service scope unless the customer purchases migration services for those specific tasks.  • After the upgrade session, The customer must upgrade all other FileCatalyst client sites.  • The customer will route all FileCatalyst related traffic from existing FileCatalyst deployment to new FileCatalyst deployment by the end of the implementation session.  • The customer may contact FileCatalyst Technical Support for any issues found after the implementation session unless dedicated cutover assistance is purchased.  • Fortra is not responsible for any connectivity issues with non-FileCatalyst systems. Fortra highly recommends performing UAT to minimize possible connectivity issues with other systems which depend on the FileCatalyst Deployment.

42029f5c-0d01-ec11-811a-0050569f6bb1	c9e51445-bff6-eb11-811a-0050569f6bb1	FileCatalyst Direct Upgrade Services	FC09023	English	FileCatalyst Direct Upgrade Services  Objective  Perform an in-place upgrade (same OS and hardware) to a newer version for up to one (1) of each applicable FileCatalyst license in a pre-existing FileCatalyst system installation using a FileCatalyst supported OS. Review of the upgrade strategy plan, risks, and compatibility.   Deliverables  • Planning sessions (remote conference and email). • One-time Implementation session (after-hours included). • Upgrade of software for one (1) of each applicable FileCatalyst license. • Post-upgrade validation checklist.  Scope of Services   The consultant will perform the following tasks:   Planning   • Plan, discuss, and review a strategy plan, risks, and compatibility for the FileCatalyst upgrade. (1 hour)   Implementation    • Backup of all applicable configurations. • Software upgrade for one (1) of each applicable FileCatalyst license and client.  • Upgrade of one (1) FileCatalyst Direct Server License to the latest version. • Upgrade of one (1) FileCatalyst TransferAgent client to the latest version (if applicable). • Upgrade of one (1) FileCatalyst HotFolder client to the latest supported version (if applicable). • Upgrade of up to one (1) FileCatalyst Reverse Proxy license to latest version (if applicable).  Validation and Testing  • Validate connectivity between FileCatalyst applications. • Perform FileCatalyst pre-defined validation & testing procedures. • Test all protocols connectivity and validate reporting is working. • Conduct basic troubleshooting of the FileCatalyst configuration for issues related to the service. • Assist with up to one (1) rollback to a previously implemented version of FileCatalyst (if needed).  Requirements  • Perform User Acceptance Testing (“UAT”) on a separate FileCatalyst environment (e.g., test or pre-production) is highly recommended before routing traffic from existing FileCatalyst deployment to new FileCatalyst deployment. The customer is responsible for performing UAT activities unless UAT services are purchased.  • Change freeze on FileCatalyst application configuration is required as soon as the upgrade service starts until the upgrade has finished with all FileCatalyst related traffic routed to the New Deployment. The Customer is responsible for maintaining any configuration changes in both server and clients during the change freeze period.   • The Customer is responsible for coordinating and performing cutover activities to route traffic from existing FileCatalyst deployment to new FileCatalyst deployment after the UAT activities have finished unless the Customer purchases a cutover assistance service.   Assumptions   • The upgrade service assumes that the customer must reuse the same architecture and infrastructure such as hypervisor virtual machines for on-prem, or cloud provider for cloud, file storage, firewalls, Active Directory, service accounts, database, and network) used by the FileCatalyst deployment and applicable access (usernames and passwords) for each component available.  • Fortra will not perform any configuration changes such as re-configuration of folder paths, ports tasks, custom commands, post-task scripts; automation refactoring, configuration clean up as part of the Upgrade service scope unless the customer purchases migration services for those specific tasks  • After the upgrade session, the customer must upgrade all other FileCatalyst client sites.  • The customer will route all FileCatalyst related traffic from existing FileCatalyst deployment to new FileCatalyst deployment by the end of the implementation session.  • The customer may contact FileCatalyst Technical Support for any issues found after the implementation session unless dedicated cutover assistance is purchased.  • Fortra is not responsible for any connectivity issues with non-FileCatalyst systems. Fortra highly recommends performing UAT to minimize possible connectivity issues with other systems which depend on the FileCatalyst Deployment.

Deliverables  • Planning checklist document. • One-time Implementation session. Installation in the target environment, migration of configuration from the source environment to target environment.  • One-time handoff session. • Service delivery documentation (Architecture Diagram, configuration report).   Scope of Services   The consultant will perform the following tasks:   Planning (remote conference and email)  • Validate all prerequisites in a pre-check remote session.  • Review necessary licensing of FileCatalyst Servers and provide perpetual license for Target environment.  • Review and validate firewall rules necessary for a secured file transfer for external and internal communication.  • Review and validate communication to file shares and database (if applicable).  • Validate path access to file share locations for internal and external users.  • Create or update an architecture diagram.  Implementation     • Install applicable software applications in the Target environment (see chart below).  • Register/Activate perpetual license.  • Migrate User configuration for user defaults, groups, folders, Active Directory settings, security policies, and Web Branding. • Reconfigure folder paths to new storage (if applicable). • Provide perpetual licensing for all target components, which enables all purchased features. • Install and migrate FileCatalyst Workflow for one (1) license.  • Install and Migrate MySQL Database (if applicable).  Validation and Testing  • Test and validate connectivity in the new FC target. • Validate Internal and External communications are open and working. • Validate Load Balancing for the FC Targets (if applicable).   Final Review  • Go through final review of installation and verify setup / configuration.  • Go through security review of applicable components (e.g., TLS, SSL). • Work with Customer team to define and go through testing for internal and external communications. • Provide current process and workflows and review customer’s automation settings to look for and provide best practices (if applicable).   Requirements  • Perform User Acceptance Testing (“UAT”) on a separate non-production environment (e.g. test or pre-production) is highly recommended before routing FC traffic from the FC Source to the FC Target. The customer is responsible for performing UAT activities unless UAT services are purchased. • Change freeze on FC source configuration is required as soon as the migration service starts and until all FC traffic is routed to the FC target. The customer is responsible for maintaining any FC configuration changes in both FC source and FC target during the change freeze period. The customer may opt to cutover to the FC target without the configuration changes and address any situation it might have, or the customer will need to purchase additional migrations services to re-do the migration if the change freeze is not maintained. • The customer is responsible for coordinating and performing cutover activities to route traffic from FC source to the FC target system after the UAT activities have finished unless the Customer purchases FileCatalyst Cutover Assistance services for such work.  Assumptions   • The customer will route all FC traffic from FC Source to FC Target by the end of the implementation session.  • The customer may contact Fortra Technical Support for any issues found after the implementation session unless a dedicated cutover assistance service is purchased.  • Fortra is not responsible for any connectivity issues with non-FileCatalyst systems. Fortra highly recommends UAT to minimize possible connectivity issues with other non-FileCatalyst systems.

The customer is responsible for maintaining any configuration changes in both server and clients during the change freeze period.   • The customer is responsible for coordinating and performing cutover activities to route traffic from existing FileCatalyst deployment to new FileCatalyst deployment after the UAT activities have finished unless the customer purchases a cutover assistance service.   Assumptions   • The upgrade service assumes that the customer must reuse the same architecture and infrastructure such as hypervisor virtual machines for on-prem, or cloud provider for cloud, file storage, firewalls, Active Directory, service accounts, database, and network) used by the FileCatalyst deployment and applicable access (usernames and passwords) for each component available.  • Fortra will not perform any configuration changes such as re-configuration of folder paths, ports tasks, custom commands, post-task scripts; automation refactoring, configuration clean up as part of the Upgrade service scope unless the customer purchases migration services for those specific tasks.  • After the upgrade session, The customer must upgrade all other FileCatalyst client sites.  • The customer will route all FileCatalyst related traffic from existing FileCatalyst deployment to new FileCatalyst deployment by the end of the implementation session.  • The customer may contact FileCatalyst Technical Support for any issues found after the implementation session unless dedicated cutover assistance is purchased.  • Fortra is not responsible for any connectivity issues with non-FileCatalyst systems. Fortra highly recommends performing UAT to minimize possible connectivity issues with other systems which depend on the FileCatalyst Deployment.

64029f5c-0d01-ec11-811a-0050569f6bb1	ebe51445-bff6-eb11-811a-0050569f6bb1	GoAnywhere EDI Specialty Certification	LN02024	English	GoAnywhere MFT Training Services  Objective  Provide training to teach GoAnywhere Managed File Transfer (“GAMFT”) based on a training agenda. A dedicated GAMFT technical consultant will deliver the training.  Deliverables  • Invitations for training to the attendees.  • One-time training session(s).   Scope of Services   The consultant will perform only the following tasks:  • Conduct training activities for the customer according to the pre-defined agenda of each training course.   Assumptions   • Each training course has a pre-defined agenda and can be found at https://www.goanywhere.com/services/product-training/course-list  • Any changes in the training agenda must be clearly defined and agreed upon in writing before the training between Fortra and the customer. • Fortra will deliver a training course in a single session. Any agenda modifications or adjustments must be requested before the service starts. • The technical consultant will use presentation materials to deliver this service. We may complement different training techniques upon request, such as interactive demo (where applicable), hands-on approach, and Q&A to complete or enhance service delivery. • Each training course has a specific number of sessions that last up to 4 hours or half-days. Each session will allocate time for introductions, a short presentation to cover core concepts, a demonstration, and Q&A. You must schedule each session within one month apart to ensure continuity and get the best learning experience. • Recordings are not allowed. We might record sessions for internal quality purposes only. Customer agrees that any unauthorized recording, reproduction, distribution, or use of any content is strictly prohibited unless Fortra explicitly authorizes it. We request not to disclose sensitive information about customer data during the training. Fortra is not responsible for any information shared during the training session by the customer. If data was unintentionally disclosed during the training, the customer is responsible for requesting to destroy any recordings to Fortra within 24 hours.

27a340eb-002e-ed11-9db1-0022481edc3a	2e8bf515-7ba1-e811-80f0-0050569f6bb1	GoAnywhere Health Check - Cluster Configuration	LN01918	English	Objective Gather environmental information of a GoAnywhere Manage File Transfer (“GAMFT”) system and generate a report on best practices for configuration and security.  How it Works We will deliver the service in three steps: 1. Information gathering: A Technical Consultant will do a shared session with you for the purpose of capturing licensed features, configuration settings, and screen grabs of non-sensitive data in the production environment. The consultant will grab logs and advanced reports to learn more about your volume and priority licensed features. (online) 2. Report Creation: The consultant will take the logs, reports, and configuration screen gatherings to compile a report on the findings. The consultant evaluates errors and pain points to offer best practices in a compiled pdf report that is securely emailed to you when complete. (offline) 3. Report Review: Once the report is completed and your team has reviewed the report, the consultant will offer a review of the high priority items in the list. This is a consultant analysis and recommendation on action items.  Deliverables • Information Gathering session(s) (Remote conference) • Client specific Health Check PDF Report (offline)          o Overall review of environment with recommendations and action items          o Security Settings Audit compliance report items (Pass/Fail) • One time discussion on priority items to implement. (Remote conference)  Scope of Services The consultant will perform the following tasks:  Environment information gathering (remote conference and email) • Review current versions of the software GAMFT, DMZ Gateway Server(s), Agent(s) • Cluster xml settings and nodes • Review all Admin Server configurations on each node for best practice configurations • Licensed Features • Review Java version and memory allocation to max heap • Review Hardware specs of the Operating System(s) • Database statistics • Key Stores (File Based or KMS) • SSL and SSH key hosting review • DoS attack, Brute-Force, Malicious username analysis • Service Listener configuration review • Job Logs – top errors, log settings • Top workflow projects with longest time execution (not redesign, only review) • Monitor, Trigger, and Scheduler engine balance review  Report Creation • Custom built report to evaluate specific environmental recommendations • OS specs and Database configuration • Licensed Features • Current Version and relation to latest GA Release • Detailed Observations          o Observations of positive configuration settings          o Observations of weak configuration settings • File Transfer volume review • Common Error log review • Clustered Node logs review • Security Settings Audit Report          o Passed|Failed|Fatal|Not Applicable • Additional Recommendations  Report Review • Consultant will review the priority items for change with your team. • Consultant will give feedback on best practice for change management implementation on items.  Assumptions • Client will share production environment in screen sharing session to allow consultant to review. • Client will share important logs and reports with consultant to complete Health Check report. • Exclusions – the following are considered out of scope with a health check.          a. Review of all workflow projects.          b. Troubleshooting poor performance issues.          c. Root Cause Analysis of a production impacting issue.          d. Upgrade of system.          e. Implementation of items on the health check.

28029f5c-0d01-ec11-811a-0050569f6bb1	afe51445-bff6-eb11-811a-0050569f6bb1	GoAnywhere MFT Core QuickStart Services	LN02003	English	GoAnywhere MFT Core QuickStart Services  Objective  Plan, advise, install, configure, and validate a GoAnywhere Manage File Transfer (“GAMFT”) system.   How it Works  We will deliver the services in four steps:  1. Planning: our Technical Consultant will review and discuss the architecture and prerequisites for your implementation. Also, we will discuss your use cases and security requirements for the optimal configuration. We will also discuss the steps needed and dates for your go-live.  2. Installation and Configuration: This includes activating the license, installation, and configuration of features according to your use cases and the QuickStart purchased (see table below).  3. Validation: Once the configuration is complete, we will work with you to validate your GAMFT deployment is working correctly. We will test protocols for internal and external connectivity, validate the database and confirm storage. And finally, we will perform a multi-point inspection checklist for production readiness and go-live.  4. Handover and review: Once the GAMFT installation and configuration are completed by the consultant, we will hand it over to you and train your team on basic administration and operations best practices.   Deliverables  • Planning checklist.  • Tailored installation and configuration of GAMFT aligned with best practices and optimal settings.  • Service delivery documentation (Architecture Diagram, GAMFT configuration report).   Scope of Services   The consultant will perform the following tasks:   Planning  • Review necessary licensing of GAMFT and DMZ Gateway Servers.  • Review and validate firewall rules necessary for secured file transfers for external and internal communication.  • Review and validate communication to file shares and database.  • Validate path access to file share locations for internal and external users.  • Create or update an architecture diagram (Premium and Enterprise Quick Start only).  • Validate all prerequisites in a pre-check remote session.   Implementation     • Registration of GAMFT license and built-in modules.  • Configure Encrypted Folders.  • Admin Authentication configuration.  • Install and configuration of GoAnywhere nodes (up to 1).   • Install and configure GoAnywhere Gateway (up to 1).  • Web users setting configuration (Templates, basic groups, users, and security policies), Web Branding.  • Configure MFT Services (SFTP, FTPS, HTTPS).  • Manual provisioning of accounts (up to 25) or automatic provisioning using a CSV file.  • Configuration of GoDrive and set up accounts (up to 5).  • Configuration of Secure Mail and setup of accounts (up to 2) to use Secure Mail.   Validation and Testing  • Validate GAMFT cluster, Database and storage is working properly.  • Test all protocols for internal and external connectivity.  • Production Readiness and Go-Live Review.  • Conduct basic troubleshooting of GAMFT configuration for issues related to the service.   Assumptions • Simple Workflow: MFT Project within GAMFT has up to five (5) simple tasks (file system, file transfer, email & SMS, compression, and PGP) only. • Advanced Workflow: MFT Project within GAMFT has up to ten (10) simple or advanced tasks (Cloud, Database, Integration, MQ, Reports, Web Services) with control and loops.  • Custom tasks or task development is excluded from Quickstart.

Assist with a dedicated technical consultant for a one-time session up to four (4) hours for a cutover or go-live event to route GoAnywhere MFT (“GAMFT”) traffic to a new GAMFT system.  Deliverables • Planning sessions (remote conference and email). • Cutover Runbook plan. • One-time pre-check review session. • One-time dedicated assistance for up to four (4) hours (after-hours included).  Scope of Services   The consultant will perform the following tasks:   Pre-Cutover Assistance  • Create, review, and validate the cutover plan. • Up to two (2) hours of dedicated assistance to copy GAMFT user data (user’s files) before the cutover. • One-time pre-check review session before the cutover assistance to analyze and validate any tracked or untracked changes on the GAMFT during the change freeze period.  Cutover Assistance  • Provide one-time dedicated assistance for up to four (4) hours (after-hours included) for the cutover and monitor and verify the new environment.  Requirements  • Change freeze on GAMFT configuration is required as soon as the upgrade service starts and until the cutover or go-live is completed. The customer is responsible for maintaining any changes made in both the source and target GAMFT system during the change freeze period.  Assumptions • Consultant may assist with minor configuration changes (if any) during change freeze period within the pre-check review session only. Assistance beyond this session will require the Customer to purchase additional professional services. • Cutover assistance deliverables are provided for the assistance during the cutover event only. Customer can purchase additional services if any work is needed due to circumstances non-attributable to Fortra or changes in the scope of the services.

2e029f5c-0d01-ec11-811a-0050569f6bb1	b5e51445-bff6-eb11-811a-0050569f6bb1	GoAnywhere MFT Enterprise QuickStart Services	LN02006	English	GoAnywhere MFT Enterprise QuickStart Services  Objective  Plan, advise, install, configure, and validate a GoAnywhere Manage File Transfer (“GAMFT”) system.   How it Works We will deliver the services in four steps: 1. Planning: our Technical Consultant will review and discuss the architecture and prerequisites for your implementation. Also, we will discuss your use cases and security requirements for the optimal configuration. We will also discuss the steps needed and dates for your go-live. 2. Installation and Configuration: This includes activating the license, installation, and configuration of features according to your use cases and the QuickStart purchased (see table below). 3. Validation: Once the configuration is complete, we will work with you to validate your GAMFT deployment is working correctly. We will test protocols for internal and external connectivity, validate the database and confirm storage. And finally, we will perform a multi-point inspection checklist for production readiness and go-live. 4. Handover and review: Once the GAMFT installation and configuration are completed by the consultant, we will hand it over to you and train your team on basic administration and operations best practices.  Deliverables  • Planning checklist. • Tailored installation and configuration of GAMFT aligned with best practices and optimal settings. • Service delivery documentation (Architecture Diagram, GAMFT configuration report).   Scope of Services   The consultant will perform the following tasks:   Planning (remote conference and email)  • Review necessary licensing of GAMFT and DMZ Gateway Servers.  • Review and validate firewall rules necessary for secured file transfers for external and internal communication.  • Review and validate communication to file shares and database.  • Validate path access to file share locations for internal and external users.  • Create or update an architecture diagram. • Validate all prerequisites in a pre-check remote session.   Implementation     • Registration of GAMFT license and built-in modules. • Configure Encrypted Folders. • Admin Authentication configuration. • Install and configuration of GAMFT nodes (up to 4). • Install and configure of GAMFT Gateways (up to 4). • Configuration and testing for up to 15 resources. • Setup Host PGP key pair and import up to 5 partner public PGP Keys. • Web users setting configuration (Templates, basic groups, users, and security policies), Web Branding  • Setup ICAP project. • Configure MFT Services (SFTP, FTPS, HTTPS). • Configure up to five (5) simple workflows and up to five (5) advanced workflows. • Manual provisioning of accounts (up to 25) or automatic provisioning using a CSV file. • Configure Agent Projects with up to ten (10) tasks each. • Configuration of GoDrive and set up accounts (up to 5). • Configuration of Secure Mail and setup of accounts (up to 2) to use Secure Mail. • Configure Standard Agents (up to 4) and Advanced Agents (up to 4). • Configure Secure Forms (up to 2). • Configure of Standard Cloud Connectors (up to 2). • Configure up to one (1) Premium Cloud Connector.  Validation and Testing  • Validate GAMFT cluster, Database and storage is working properly.  • Test all protocols for internal and external connectivity.  • Production Readiness and Go-Live Review  • Conduct basic troubleshooting of GAMFT configuration for issues related to the service.   Assumptions  • Simple Workflow: MFT Project within GAMFT has up to five (5) simple tasks (file system, file transfer, email & SMS, compression, and PGP) only.  • Advanced Workflow: MFT Project within GAMFT has up to ten (10) simple or advanced tasks (Cloud, Database, Integration, MQ, Reports, Web Services) with control and loops.  • Custom tasks or task development and EDI Configuration is excluded from Quickstart.

32029f5c-0d01-ec11-811a-0050569f6bb1	b9e51445-bff6-eb11-811a-0050569f6bb1	GoAnywhere MFT In-Place Upgrade Services	LN02008	English	"GoAnywhere In-Place Upgrade Services Objective Review of the upgrade strategy plan, risks, and compatibility. Perform an In-Place Upgrade on a pre-existing GoAnywhere Managed File Transfer (“GAMFT”) system using a GAMFT supported Operating System.   Deliverables •	Planning sessions (remote conference and email). •	One-time Implementation session (after-hours included). •	Upgrade of software for one (1) GoAnywhere Environment (e.g. Prod, Dev, or Test). •	Post-upgrade validation checklist.  Scope of Service The consultant will perform the following tasks:  Planning  •	Plan, discuss, and review a strategy plan, risks, and compatibility for the upgrade.  Implementation   •	Backup/Restore of all applicable configurations. •	Upgrade of one (1) GAMFT Environment to the latest version. (Includes all nodes of a single environment) •	Upgrade of one (1) GAMFT Agent to the latest version (if applicable). •	Upgrade of up to 2 GoAnywhere Gateway(s) to the latest supported version - Gateway(s) connected to a single environment (if applicable).   Validation and Testing •	Validate connectivity between GAMFT applications. •	Perform pre-defined validation & testing procedures.  •	Test all protocols connectivity and validate reporting is working. •	Conduct basic troubleshooting of the GAMFT configuration for issues related to the service. •	Assist with up to one (1) rollback to the previously implemented version of GAMFT (if needed).  Requirements •	Perform User Acceptance Testing (“UAT”) on a separate GAMFT environment (e.g., test or pre-production) is highly recommended before routing traffic to GAMFT. The customer is responsible for performing UAT activities unless UAT services are purchased. •	Change freeze on GAMFT configuration is required as soon as the upgrade service starts until the upgrade has finished.  Assumptions  •	The customer acknowledges that same architecture and infrastructure such as hypervisor for virtual machines for on-prem, or cloud provider for cloud, file storage, firewalls, Active Directory, service accounts, database, and network and the applicable access control will be re-used. •	Fortra will not perform any configuration changes such as re-configuration of folder paths, ports tasks, custom commands, post-task scripts; automation refactoring, configuration clean up as part of the Upgrade service scope unless migration services are purchased for those specific tasks. •	The customer may contact GAMFT Technical Support for any issues found after the implementation session unless dedicated cutover assistance is purchased. •	GAMFT is not responsible for any connectivity issues with non- GAMFT systems. Fortra highly recommends performing UAT to minimize possible connectivity issues with other systems which may depend on parts of the GAMFT deployment."

30029f5c-0d01-ec11-811a-0050569f6bb1	b7e51445-bff6-eb11-811a-0050569f6bb1	GoAnywhere MFT Migration Services	LN02007	English	GoAnywhere MFT Migration Service Plan, install, upgrade, migrate GoAnywhere Managed File Transfer (“GAMFT”) configuration from a pre-installed GAMFT (“GAMFT Source”) into a brand new GAMFT (“GAMFT Target”). Reconfiguring folder paths, resources and upgrade to a newer supported GAMFT version.  How it Works We will deliver the services in four steps: 1. Planning: our Technical Consultant will review and discuss the architecture and prerequisites for your implementation. We will also discuss the steps needed and dates for your go-live. 2. Installation and Migration: This includes installation, activating the license, configuration and migration of features from your GAMFT source. 3. Validation: Once the migration is completed, we will confirm your GAMFT deployment is working. We will test protocols for internal and external connectivity, validate the database and confirm storage. And finally, we will perform a multi-point inspection checklist for production readiness and go-live and handover to you for the UAT or Cutover.  Deliverables • Planning sessions (remote conference and email). • Planning Checklist. • One-time implementation session to install and migrate one (1) GAMFT configuration. • Service delivery documentation. • What’s new notes.  Scope of Services The consultant will perform the following tasks:  Planning • Plan, discuss, and review a strategy plan, risks, and compatibility for the GAMFT Migration. • Analysis of configuration for projects, schedules, monitors, triggers and resources to determine necessary changes to scripting access, file movements and transfers based on local folder access to network share access to accommodate the usage of clustered MFT instances.  Implementation • Registration of GAMFT license and built-in modules. • Install and configure GAMFT nodes (up to 4). • Install and configure GAMFT Gateways (up to 4). • Upgrade GAMFT configuration to a newer supported GAMFT version. • Migrate/Consolidate (Promote) settings from GAMFT Source to GAMFT Target: 	• Resources, Monitors, Scheduled Jobs, Keys & Certificates, Web Users, Templates, Groups. • Resources, Monitors, Scheduled Jobs, Keys & Certificates, Web Users, Templates, Groups. • Reconfigure new folder paths for GAMFT as needed. • Assist for up to two (2) hours in copying user’s files from the GAMFT source to the GAMFT target folder.  Validation and Testing  • Test and validate connectivity in the new GAMFT target. • Validate DMZ Gateway connections from Internal and External communications are open and working. • Validate High Availability for the GAMFT Target clustering transfer (Coordinator vs Participant) and verify that control is maintained when communication is interrupted, and the controller switch, as necessary (if HA is available).  Final Review  • Go through final review of installation and verify setup / configuration. • Go through security review (SSH/TLS). • Go through black-listing review – validate status as defined by development review and suggested modifications. • Work with Customer team to define and go through testing for internal and external communications. • Provide current process and workflows and review Customer’s automation settings to look for and provide best practices.  Requirements  • GAMFT source and GAMFT target must be installed and validated by the Customer unless QuickStart services are purchased. • Perform User Acceptance Testing (“UAT”) on a separate GAMFT environment (e.g. test or pre-production) is highly recommended before routing GAMFT traffic from the GAMFT Source to the GAMFT Target. The Customer is responsible for performing UAT activities unless UAT services are purchased. • Change freeze on GAMFT source configuration is required as soon as the migration service starts and until all GAMFT traffic is routed to the GAMFT target. The Customer is responsible for maintaining any GAMFT configuration changes in both GAMFT source and GAMFT target during the change freeze period. The Customer may opt to cutover to the GAMFT target without the configuration changes and address any situation it might have or, the Customer will need to purchase an additional GAMFT Migrations to re-do the migration if the change freeze is not maintained. • The Customer is responsible for coordinating and performing cutover activities to route GAMFT traffic from GAMFT source to the GAMFT target system after the UAT activities have finished unless the Customer purchases GAMFT cutover assistance services for such work.  Assumptions   • The customer will route all GAMFT traffic from GAMFT Source to GAMFT Target by the end of the implementation session. • The Customer may contact Fortra Technical Support for any issues found after the implementation session unless dedicated cutover assistance service is purchased • Fortra is not responsible for any connectivity issues with non-GAMFT systems. Fortra highly recommends UAT to minimize possible connectivity issues with other non-GAMFT systems.

(e.g. Prod, Dev, or Test).    •	Post-migration validation.  Scope of Service The consultant will perform the following tasks:  Planning  •	Plan, discuss, and review a strategy plan, risks, and compatibility for the upgrade of legacy and move to new Server(s).  Implementation   •	Backup/Restore of all applicable configurations. •	Upgrade of one (1) GAMFT Envrionment to the latest version. (Includes all nodes of a single environment) •	Upgrade of one (1) GAMFT Agent to the latest version (if applicable). •	Upgrade or Move of up to 2 GoAnywhere Gateway(s) to the latest supported version - Gateway(s) connected to a single environment (if applicable).  •	Install of one (1) GAMFT Environment with the latest version. •	Instruct what files to copy to new repository. (userdata files)   •	Point new GAMFT version to replicated database or existing database.  Validation and Testing •	Validate connectivity between GAMFT applications. •	Perform pre-defined validation & testing procedures.  •	Test all protocols connectivity and validate reporting is working. •	Conduct basic troubleshooting of the GAMFT configuration for issues related to the service.  Requirements •	Perform User Acceptance Testing (“UAT”) on a separate GAMFT environment (e.g., test or pre-production) is highly recommended before routing traffic to GAMFT. The customer is responsible for performing UAT activities unless UAT services are purchased. •	Change freeze on GAMFT configuration is required as soon as the upgrade service starts until the migration to new server has finished.  Assumptions  •	The environment GAMFT is moving to is the same vendor of operating system.  This is not a cross-OS upgrade.  That would be considered a migration service based on Operating System differences. (Example: Windows 2012 server on legacy server -> Windows 2019 Server on newer environment)  Windows -> Windows, Linux -> Linux, IBMi -> IBMi. •	Copying of the files will be the customer responsibility.  The consultant will instruct on location, but the responsibility for moving the files needed in new environment is with the customer. •	A cutover purchase is suggested to assist with the Go-Live portion of the new environment.  Cutover should align closely with the migration of data to avoid data loss between legacy and new environment. •	It is recommended the upgrade of legacy environment is done in phase 1 and the OS move is done in phase 2.  In order to validate the upgrade has not caused any unintended setbacks.  Typically 1 week-4 week period based on client’s priority project scheduling in the application. •	The customer acknowledges the new architecture and infrastructure such as hypervisor for virtual machines for on-prem, or cloud provider for cloud, file storage, firewalls, Active Directory, service accounts, database, and network and the applicable access control will be the same in new environment. •	Fortra will not perform any configuration changes such as re-configuration of folder paths, ports tasks, custom commands, post-task scripts; automation refactoring, configuration clean up as part of the Upgrade /Move service scope unless migration services are purchased for those specific tasks. •	The customer will route all GAMFT related traffic from existing GAMFT deployment to new GAMFT deployment by the end of the implementation session. •	The customer may contact GAMFT Technical Support for any issues found after the implementation session unless dedicated cutover assistance is purchased. •	GAMFT is not responsible for any connectivity issues with non- GAMFT systems. Fortra highly recommends performing UAT to minimize possible connectivity issues with other systems which may depend on parts of the GAMFT deployment."

2c029f5c-0d01-ec11-811a-0050569f6bb1	b3e51445-bff6-eb11-811a-0050569f6bb1	GoAnywhere MFT Premium QuickStart Services	LN02005	English	GoAnywhere MFT Premium QuickStart Services  Objective  Plan, advise, install, configure, and validate a GoAnywhere Manage File Transfer (“GAMFT”) system.   How it Works  We will deliver the services in four steps:   1. Planning: our Technical Consultant will review and discuss the architecture and prerequisites for your implementation. Also, we will discuss your use cases and security requirements for the optimal configuration. We will also discuss the plan steps needed and dates for your go-live. 2. Installation and Configuration: This includes activating the license, installation, and configuration of features according to your use cases and the QuickStart purchased (see table below).  3. Validation: Once the configuration is complete, we will work with you to validate your GAMFT deployment is working correctly. We will test protocols for internal and external connectivity, validate the database and confirm storage. And finally, we will perform a multi-point inspection checklist for production readiness and go-live.  4. Handover and review: Once the GAMFT installation and configuration are completed by the consultant, we will hand it over to you and train your team on basic administration and operations best practices.   Deliverables  • Planning checklist   • Tailored installation and configuration of GAMFT aligned with best practices and optimal settings  • Service delivery documentation (Architecture Diagram, GAMFT configuration report).   Scope of Services   The consultant will perform the following tasks:   Planning (remote conference and email)  • Review necessary licensing of GAMFT and DMZ Gateway Servers.  • Review and validate firewall rules necessary for secured file transfers for external and internal communication.  • Review and validate communication to file shares and database.  • Validate path access to file share locations for internal and external users.  • Create or update an architecture diagram. • Validate all prerequisites in a pre-check remote session.   Implementation     • Registration of GAMFT license and built-in modules.  • Configure Encrypted Folders.  • Admin Authentication configuration.  • Install and configure GAMFT nodes (up to 4).  • Install and configure GAMFT Gateways (up to 4).  • Configuration and testing for up to 10 resources.  • Setup Host PGP key pair and Import partner public PGP Key (up to 5).  • Web users setting configuration (Templates, basic groups, users, and security policies), Web Branding.  • Configure MFT Services (SFTP, FTPS, HTTPS).  • Configure up to five (5) simple workflows.  • Configure up to five (5) advanced workflows.  • Manual provisioning of accounts (up to 25) or automatic provisioning using a CSV file.  • Configure Agent Projects with up to ten (10) tasks each.  • Configuration of GoDrive and set up accounts (up to 5)  • Configuration of Secure Mail and setup of accounts (up to 2) to use Secure Mail.  • Configure Standard Agents (up to 4).  • Configure Secure Forms (up to 2).  • Configure of Standard Cloud Connectors (up to 2).  • Configure up to one (1) AS2 partner.   Validation and Testing  • Validate GAMFT cluster, Database and storage is working properly.  • Test all protocols for internal and external connectivity.  • Production Readiness and Go-Live Review  • Conduct basic troubleshooting of GAMFT configuration for issues related to the service.   Assumptions  • Simple Workflow: MFT Project within GAMFT has up to five (5) simple tasks (file system, file transfer, email & SMS, compression, and PGP) only.  • Advanced Workflow: MFT Project within GAMFT has up to ten (10) simple or advanced tasks (Cloud, Database, Integration, MQ, Reports, Web Services) with control and loops.  • Custom tasks or task development is excluded from Quickstart.

If data was unintentionally disclosed during the training, the customer is responsible for requesting to destroy any recordings to Fortra within 24 hours. • Each quarter Fortra will publish a schedule for Public ILT (Instructor-led Training) services. The customer can sign up and attend these training sessions. The Public ILT training will be shared along with other customers. Help Systems charges per seat for each course. Public ILT is delivered remote via web conference and there is a limit of 25 attendees per training. The customer will have up to one year to attend. The customer must pre-register within a week before the training.

Public ILT is delivered remote via web conference and there is a limit of 25 attendees per training. The customer will have up to one year to attend. The customer must pre-register within a week before the training.

How it Works We will deliver the services in four steps:  1.	Planning: Our Technical Consultant will review and discuss the architecture and prerequisites for your implementation. We will discuss JAMS licensing and JAMS Environments. Also, we will discuss your use cases and security requirements for the optimal configuration. We will also discuss the steps needed and dates for your go-live. 2.	Installation and Configuration: This includes activating the license, installation, and configuration of features according to your use cases. 3.	Validation: Once the configuration is complete, we will work with you to validate your JAMS deployment is working correctly. We will validate our actions with a sample bot testing internal connectivity, validating the database, and confirming storage. 4.	Job building and Handover: Once the JAMS installation and configuration are completed by the consultant, we will select up to 50 jobs of any of the proposed types: Scripts, FTP, SQL, 3rd party, sequences.  Deliverables •	Planning checklist. •	Tailored installation and configuration of JAMS aligned with best practices and optimal settings. •	Service delivery documentation (Administrator document & Implementation report). Scope of Services  The consultant will perform the following tasks:  Planning (remote conference and email) •	Meet your Project Manager (your primary contact for implementation) and Implementation Consultant •	Discuss your processes and requirements with the Project Manager •	Provide detailed information about your systems and processes. •	Subscribe to the customer portal (Access to latest releases, documentation, and support) •	Subscribe to JAMS Academy  •	Review necessary licensing of JAMS. •	Review and define architecture diagram. •	Review communication to file shares and database. •	Conduct a simple test job on local server for health check. •	Validate all prerequisites in a kickoff remote session. Installation/Configuration •	Define and Install Agents •	Deploy Agents from Client or install agents manually. •	Install PowerShell 3.0+, SQL SMO, SQL SDK Backwards compatibility, and .NET 4.5. •	Define SMTP Server. •	Set default notification email address. •	Set From Address. •	Create Firewall Rules for JAMS Server, Agents, and Clients. •	Create JAMS User Definitions. •	Define Security Roles within JAMS. •	Configure Client for multiple JAMS environments.  •	Select theme for each environment.  •	Configure Dashboards. Implementation    •	Organized by Application? Server? Platform? Department? •	Establish a naming convention. •	Define Service Accounts in AD then JAMS for jobs ""Run As"" accounts. •	Group jobs in Sequences\Workflows. •	Define Batch Queues. •	Create Custom Calendars. •	Establish Job / Setup Schedule Date(s). •	Set Job Recurrence options. •	Configure systems jobs that store JAMS History and Audit. •	Establish a protocol for moving jobs between environments. •	Process discovery •	Initial Implementation o	Scripts- Identify paths to scripts to Reference in Jobs. - Pull script source directly into JAMS Jobs. o	SQL Jobs - Configure Connection Strings at the job and folder level. - Unique Execution Methods o	FTP Jobs - Using the File Transfer Execution method - PowerShell FTP Documentation - Workflow FTP Activities o	Convert Native and Proprietary Jobs - SQL Agent - Task Scheduler - Cron - Export Cron tabs - Convert utilizing Cron Converter Utility - 3rd Party Scheduler o	Group jobs in Sequences\Workflows •	Test Jobs - Submit Jobs and Sequences manually. Validation and Testing •	Project management •	Production Readiness and Go-Live Review •	Conduct basic troubleshooting of JAMS configuration for issues related to the service. •	Define who receives notifications for various event in JAMS. •	Add Recovery Rules to Jobs - Set Retry Counts and Duration. - Add Recovery Instructions. - Define Recovery Jobs. •	SCOM - Download and import SCOM Management Pack. •	SNMP Trapping - Download and configure SNMP MIB file.

Department? •	Establish a naming convention. •	Define Service Accounts in AD then JAMS for jobs ""Run As"" accounts. •	Group jobs in Sequences\Workflows. •	Define Batch Queues. •	Create Custom Calendars. •	Establish Job / Setup Schedule Date(s). •	Set Job Recurrence options. •	Configure systems jobs that store JAMS History and Audit. •	Establish a protocol for moving jobs between environments. •	Process discovery •	Initial Implementation o	Scripts- Identify paths to scripts to Reference in Jobs. - Pull script source directly into JAMS Jobs. o	SQL Jobs - Configure Connection Strings at the job and folder level. - Unique Execution Methods o	FTP Jobs - Using the File Transfer Execution method - PowerShell FTP Documentation - Workflow FTP Activities o	Convert Native and Proprietary Jobs - SQL Agent - Task Scheduler - Cron - Export Cron tabs - Convert utilizing Cron Converter Utility - 3rd Party Scheduler o	Group jobs in Sequences\Workflows •	Test Jobs - Submit Jobs and Sequences manually. Validation and Testing •	Project management •	Production Readiness and Go-Live Review •	Conduct basic troubleshooting of JAMS configuration for issues related to the service. •	Define who receives notifications for various event in JAMS. •	Add Recovery Rules to Jobs - Set Retry Counts and Duration. - Add Recovery Instructions. - Define Recovery Jobs. •	SCOM - Download and import SCOM Management Pack. •	SNMP Trapping - Download and configure SNMP MIB file.

7c3c845d-3abb-ec11-983f-0022482f0623	13e4566f-38bb-ec11-983f-0022482f0623	QSECURITY 30 to QSECURITY 40 - Security Level Change Feasibility Assessment Service	PT02108	English	Assess the feasibility of moving from QSECURITY 30 to QSECURITY 40 as follows:   •	Provide guidance on the testing that needs to be performed prior to moving the system to security level 40  •	Provide instructions on the system value configuration required to enable auditing prior to moving the system to security level 40 •	Assist customer in collecting audit entries that relate to moving to security level 40; this may include scheduling queries provided by Fortra that retrieve these entries •	Evaluate the results of the reports generated by these queries •	Provide guidance if issues are discovered, including guidance on options that can be taken to resolve the issues Deliverables include: •	A report summarizing guidance, evaluation results and issues to be addressed, if any. Any changes to client or vendor code or applications identified during this process to ensure a successful move to security level 40 are the responsibility of the Client.

Globalscape may supplement with additional tools as needed with approval by the Customer.</p>  <p>&#160;</p>  <p>Materials delivered by Globalscape will be constrained within the product core features and are delivered &#8220;AS IS&#8221;. Any work that requires a consultant or developer additional time of the hours will be considered out of the scope. In such situations, the customer may purchase additional services.</p>  <p>&#160;</p>  <p>Globalscape may develop or use custom tools, scripts, or may use third-party tools approved by the Customer to provide functionality when necessary throughout the project. Development of custom code or scripting is provided &#8220;AS IS&#8221; and Globalscape will not provide maintenance or support for such code. Any modification, issues/defects, configuration, assistance, usage, troubleshooting, or documentation shall require an additional professional services engagement in a separate statement of work.</p>  <p>&#160;</p>  <p>Globalscape has prepared the above estimate of hours in good faith for Customer&#8217;s planning and budgeting purposes based upon the information available to Globalscape. Changed assumptions or circumstances, changes in the objectives or scope of the project, and/or new information acquired during the project, may require additional efforts on the part of Globalscape.&#160; Customer will pay Globalscape for such additional efforts at the Cost set forth above.&#160; If Customer or Globalscape become aware of circumstances that are likely to cause the Cost to be exceeded, such as changed assumptions or circumstances, changes in the project objectives or scope and/or new information acquired during the course of the project, Customer and Globalscape can negotiate a mutually acceptable change order modifying the description of the Services and/or the cost estimates.</p>  <p>&#160;</p>  <p>Scheduled remote working sessions must be scheduled in continuous blocks of time with a minimum of 1-hour blocks for on-demand ( consultation, meetings, planning, etc) service requests or a minimum of 4-hour (or more) blocks.</p>  <p>&#160;</p>  <p>We do our best to accommodate schedule changes, however we require a minimum of 24-hour notice for scheduled 1-hour block and a minimum of 5 business days notice for 4-hours block. If a cancellation is made within the minimum window, or in the event of a no-show, we reserve the right to charge those scheduled hours.</p>
a2X4v00000GC24OEAT	PS Accelerators	<p style="text-align: center;"><span style="font-size:14px;"><strong>PS EFT Accelerators</strong></span></p>  <p>&#160;</p>  <p><strong>Objective</strong></p>  <p>One-on-one engagement with Customer to discuss business requirements and provide customized advice.</p>  <p>During the service, Globalscape will not work directly in Customer&#8217;s environment. Globalscape may build out a portion of the deliverables for a development/test environment. Globalscape shall coach/show Customer how to accomplish a feature adoption as opposed to Globalscape performing the work for the Customer.</p>  <p>Globalscape may assign a specific action plan to the Customer for adoption of the feature. A follow-up session will be scheduled within ten (10) business days of the coaching session to review the feature adoption by customer.</p>  <p>&#160;</p>  <p><strong>Deliverables</strong></p>  <ul> 	<li><strong>One</strong> (1) coaching session for a deep dive remote 1-on-1 for up to half day (remote conference).</li> 	<li><strong>One</strong> (1) final review session (one hour remote) with Customer within ten (10) business days after the coaching session.</li> 	<li>EFT Accelerator Report with an feature adoption checklist.</li> </ul>  <p>&#160;</p>  <p><strong>Scope of Services</strong></p>  <p>For each PS EFT Accelerator purchased in this quote the customer can pick <strong>One</strong> <strong>(1)</strong> topic of any of the following:</p>  <ol> 	<li><strong>Sending and sharing files using Workspaces</strong>  	<ol start="1" style="list-style-type: lower-alpha;"> 		<li>Review of use cases for Workspaces, send and request files, drop-off portal.</li> 		<li>How to use and manage Workspaces.</li> 		<li>Feature adoption checklist.</li> 	</ol> 	</li> 	<li><strong>Remote Agent module</strong> 	<ol start="1" style="list-style-type: lower-alpha;"> 		<li>Review of use cases for Remote Agents.</li> 		<li>How to install remote agents, manage profiles, use remote automation rules.</li> 		<li>Feature adoption checklist.</li> 	</ol> 	</li> 	<li><strong>Tracking and monitoring files</strong> 	<ol start="1" style="list-style-type: lower-alpha;"> 		<li>Understand ARM settings, client logs, and tracking file transfers.</li> 		<li>How to configure ARM, custom reports.</li> 		<li>Feature adoption checklist.</li> 	</ol> 	</li> 	<li><strong>Configuring Two Factor Authentication </strong> 	<ol start="1" style="list-style-type: lower-alpha;"> 		<li>Understand how Two Factor Authentication settings in EFT.</li> 		<li>How to configure, test and validate Two Factor Authentication in EFT.</li> 		<li>Feature adoption checklist.</li> 	</ol> 	</li> </ol>  <p>&#160;</p>  <p><strong>Requirements</strong></p>  <ul> 	<li>Time commitment during the coaching session from Customer personnel, such as EFT administrators, change management resources, networking, business users, etc.</li> 	<li>EFT System must be installed, correctly working, and fully functional.</li> 	<li>For all feature options, the EFT License(s) must be purchased with an applicable Maintenance and Support Plan by the Customer.</li> 	<li>Customer is required to provision and configure dependencies for non-Globalscape products.</li> </ul>  <p>&#160;</p>  <p><strong>Assumptions</strong></p>  <ul> 	<li>Globalscape will remain reachable via email for up to ten (10) business days from the coaching session for any additional inquiries.</li> 	<li>Additional remote sessions (up to thirty minutes each) may be available upon request and subject to availability.</li> </ul>
a2X4v00000Smr1NEAR	PS Hourtly TAM	<p style="text-align: center;"><span style="font-size:14px;"><strong>EFT TAM</strong></span></p>  <p><br /> &#160;</p>  <p><strong>Description</strong><br /> Globalscape Technical Account Manager (TAM) is a premium service offered to Globalscape customers and is intended to help coordinate the support and services relationship.&#160; &#160;The TAM is your advocate within Globalscape and facilitates a team that can provide problem resolution support, support assistance, and ad-hoc training. The TAM also provides your feedback regarding Globalscape software to other Globalscape groups.&#160; This service assists customers in achieving the maximum value from their enterprise deployment of Globalscape product.&#160;</p>  <p><br /> This is a well-structured engagement where regular &#8220;check-point&#8221; meetings are scheduled in advance between a designated point of contact of the customer and the designated Globalscape product expert for the duration of the Globalscape TAM term.&#160; The frequency and duration of calls is determined by the level of TAM engagement that the customer has requested and as is indicated in the Deliverable section.&#160;</p>  <p>&#160;</p>  <p><strong>Scope</strong></p>  <p>Only available in North America region (Central Time Zone)</p>  <p>The level of engagement depends on the level of TAM purchased.
